These are source map assets and historical source records. Current ELECT geometry, 2020 Census VTDs and historical result units are separate inventories. No approved leaf join, legal adoption, crossyear equivalence or spatial allocation of votes, ballots or registration is asserted. Virtual/administrative records remain visible. ELECT warns that recently enacted local changes may take weeks to appear.
